Motivation: 评分器在一个LLM prompt中同时为多个候选打分，导致每个分数依赖于候选的顺序。现有工作仅以排序质量（如nDCG）来评估这些评分器，但实际决策（阈值保留哪些文档、读者回答什么、偏好模型选择什么）由具体分数决定。排序质量相同并不保证决策相同，因此需要研究顺序依赖对决策的影响并提供解决方案。

Method: 1) 通过实证对比展示排序质量相近（差距在0.010 nDCG@10以内）的五个训练评分器，在重排后保留集重叠度仅为0.66-0.84。2) 系统测试了多种prompt层面的改动（提示改写等），发现均无法消除顺序依赖；唯一提升排序质量的改动也未能改变任何决策。3) 提出OC-SFT（Order-Consistency SFT），在权重层面训练候选分数不依赖输入顺序，以保持排序质量和提升决策稳定性，并与顺序平均蒸馏等基线在12个基础模型上对比。

Result: OC-SFT在三个任务（passage reranking、reward modeling、multi-document QA）上均在所有决策稳定性指标上领先：在reader答案翻转率上为0.125，而其他三个针对顺序的目标为0.149-0.164。OC-SFT在12个基础模型上比顺序平均蒸馏更稳定，且单个OC-SFT排列的保留集重叠度超过十个平均化的现成排列。发布的reranker在F1上最高但保留集重叠度仅0.667，远低于OC-SFT。

Conclusion: 评分器的顺序依赖是一个内在问题，prompt层面的修改无法解决，需在训练权重层面处理（OC-SFT）。评测报告应包含决策层面的指标（阈值保留集、读者答案），而不仅仅是排序质量。代码已开源。

Abstract: Rerankers, reward models and multi-document QA scorers score candidate documents or responses in one LLM prompt, so each score depends on their order. Such scorers are selected on ranking quality, but their scores determine a decision: what a score threshold retains, a reader answers, or a preference model selects. However, equal ranking quality does not imply equal decisions: on passage reranking, five trained scorers within 0.010 nDCG@10 retain sets that overlap by only 0.66-0.84 when reordered. A published reranker takes the highest retained-set F1 in our comparison and still overlaps by only 0.667. No prompt-time change we test removes that order dependence: the only one that gains ranking quality leaves all three decisions unchanged. Order-consistency SFT (OC-SFT) attenuates it in the weights, training a candidate's score not to depend on the order. It holds ranking quality and leads every decision-stability measure among trained scorers on all three tasks: it flips the reader's answer on 0.125 of permutation pairs against 0.149-0.164 for three other objectives that target order. It is more stable than order-averaged distillation on 12 base models, and one OC-SFT permutation retains sets that overlap more than ten averaged off-the-shelf permutations. A comparison should therefore report what a threshold retains and a reader answers, not ranking quality alone. Code is available at https://github.com/thomsonreuters/presentation-dependence.

</details>


### [172] [Instruction Quality Matters: Refining Instructions for Effective Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26779)
*Seohyeong Lee,Hwaran Lee,Buru Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现指令质量是偏好学习的隐藏瓶颈，并提出了一个指令精细化流水线来改进偏好数据，从而提升LLM对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好学习中响应对的丰富性受生成它们的指令质量的根本制约，而现有工作大多聚焦于响应侧的数据筛选，忽略了指令质量这一隐藏瓶颈。作者通过Best/Worst-of-N分析发现指令质量同时约束了采样响应质量的上限和下限，因此有必要从指令侧改进偏好数据。

Method: 提出一个指令精细化流水线：先用奖励信号筛选出质量较低的指令，再利用rubric引导的LLM反馈对新指令进行修订，从而改进偏好数据而不丢弃任何样本。该方法可同时用于离线和在线偏好学习场景。

Result: 在多个模型和基准上，相比原始数据及其他数据改进策略，该方法在离线和在线偏好学习中都取得了广泛的对齐性能提升。进一步分析表明，指令精细化提高了可达到的响应质量上限，并能够与以响应为中心的偏好数据筛选方法互补。

Conclusion: 指令质量是决定LLM对齐中偏好信号形成信息量的关键因素，指令精细化作为一种数据层面的干预手段，为偏好学习提供了一条新的、有效的提升路径。

Abstract: Preference learning optimizes models using response pairs, yet the informativeness of these pairs is fundamentally shaped by the instructions from which they are generated. We identify instruction quality as a hidden bottleneck in preference learning: low-quality or ambiguous instructions restrict the response-quality distribution, limiting strong chosen responses and weakening preference signals. Through Best- and Worst-of-N analyses, we show that instruction quality constrains both the ceiling and floor of sampled response quality. Motivated by this observation, we introduce an instruction-refinement pipeline that selects weak instructions using reward signals and revises them with rubric-guided LLM feedback, improving preference data without discarding examples. Across offline and online preference learning settings, experiments on multiple models and benchmarks show broad alignment improvements over original data and alternative data-improvement strategies. Further analyses indicate that instruction refinement raises achievable response quality and complements response-centric preference data curation. Overall, instruction quality emerges as a key factor governing how informative preference signals are formed for LLM alignment. Code is available at: https://github.com/01choco/instruction-refinement/

</details>


### [173] [Behavior2Trip: Towards Personalized Travel Planning via User Behavior Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26807)
*Zihao Cheng,Yingyu Shan,Hongru Wang,Zeming Liu,Xinyi Wang,Xiangrong Zhu,Yuhang Guo,Wei Lin,Yunhong Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出行为感知旅行规划（Behavior-Aware Travel Planning）新任务，并从大型中文在线旅游平台构建了包含11,400个实例的Behavior2Trip基准数据集，同时提出基于强化学习的B2T-Agent代理，通过利用用户历史行为轨迹生成个性化旅行计划，实验表明其显著优于GPT-4.1等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旅行规划代理要么依赖显式用户指令，要么通过多轮对话澄清来获取偏好，均忽略了用户历史行为中隐式编码的丰富偏好信号。这种对主动用户输入的过度依赖增加了交互负担并限制了规划的个性化程度。

Method: 提出B2T-Agent，一个基于强化学习的智能代理，利用用户行为轨迹作为输入，与外部工具交互进行偏好对齐的检索，并维护内部记忆模块来生成个性化旅行计划。同时构建Behavior2Trip基准数据集，每个实例包含平均39.8个用户历史行为，跨越5个偏好维度、14个属性。

Result: 在Behavior2Trip上，GPT-4.1在最困难任务上的完整约束通过率仅为0.5%，而基于Qwen3-8B的B2T-Agent在所有基线上表现最优。Qwen3-8B经B2T-Agent训练后在TravelPlanner基准上也优于GPT-4.1，展示了强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 行为感知旅行规划任务具有显著挑战性，基于强化学习的B2T-Agent能够有效利用历史行为隐式偏好信息，生成个性化旅行计划，且展现出良好的跨基准泛化性能。

Abstract: Travel planning agents assist users in generating personalized travel plans by modeling their individual preferences. Existing agents either rely on explicit user instructions or engage in multi-turn clarification to elicit user preferences. However, both approaches overlook the rich behavioral signals latent in users' past behaviors, which implicitly encode their preferences. This over-reliance on active user input increases interaction burden and limits plan personalization. To bridge this gap, we introduce a new task, Behavior-Aware Travel Planning, which infers user preferences directly from past behaviors and generates personalized travel plans. To facilitate research on this task, we introduce Behavior2Trip, a benchmark constructed from one of the largest Chinese online travel platforms, comprising 11,400 instances. Each instance represents an average of 39.8 past user behaviors spanning 14 attributes across 5 preference dimensions. We further propose B2T-Agent, a reinforcement learning-based agent that leverages user behavior trajectories, interacts with external tools for preference-aligned retrieval, and maintains an internal memory module. Experiments on Behavior2Trip show that GPT-4.1 achieves a full-constraint pass rate of only 0.5\% on the hardest tasks, while B2T-Agent built upon Qwen3-8B outperforms all baselines, highlighting the substantial challenge of this task. Moreover, Qwen3-8B trained with B2T-Agent also outperforms GPT-4.1 on the TravelPlanner benchmark, demonstrating strong generalization. Code and data are available at https://github.com/BUAA-IRIP-LLM/Behavior2Trip

</details>


### [174] [RuleWeaver: Benchmarking Rule-Centered Scenario Reasoning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26832)
*Bohan Yu,Shi-Yang Li,Pengfei Cao,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RuleWeaver，一个用于评估规则中心情景推理的基准构建框架。它从语料库提取IF-THEN元规则，逐步增强为复杂规则，并组合成规则中心的情景问答实例，支持过程级评估（rubric评分、规则召回率和精确率）。实验表明11个LLM在复杂规则推理上表现不佳，最佳模型仅约50%的rubric分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在专业领域的应用中，需要对复杂规则进行场景化推理。然而现有基准要么只关注输出级指令约束，要么忽视了规则在情景推理中的不同作用，无法全面评估这一关键能力。

Method: RuleWeaver框架包括三个步骤：从语料中提取IF-THEN元规则，渐进增强这些规则使其复杂化，然后将组合规则构建为规则中心的情景问答实例。评估方面不仅关注最终答案正确性，还通过基于rubric的答案质量、规则召回率和规则精确率进行过程级评估。

Result: 在11个代表性LLM上的实验显示，当前模型在复杂规则中心的情景推理上仍存在明显困难，即使是表现最好的模型也只能达到约50%的最高rubric分数。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在规则中心的复杂情景推理方面能力不足，RuleWeaver为这一能力的评估提供了更全面的基准框架，并为后续改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to specialized domains, where effective use of domain expertise often requires reasoning over complex rules in concrete scenarios. However, existing benchmarks only partially evaluate this capability, as they either focus on output-level instruction constraints or overlook the distinct roles that rules play in scenario reasoning. To address these gaps, this paper introduces RuleWeaver, a benchmark construction framework for evaluating rule-centered scenario reasoning. RuleWeaver starts from corpus-derived IF-THEN Meta Rules, progressively augments them into complex rules, and composes these rules into rule-centered scenario QA instances. Beyond final-answer correctness, RuleWeaver further supports process-level evaluation through rubric-based answer quality, rule recall, and rule precision. Experiments on 11 representative LLMs show that current models still struggle with complex rule-centered scenario reasoning, with even the best-performing model achieving only around 50% of the maximum rubric score. We make our code and dataset available here: https://github.com/SharkSpicy-NLP/RuleWeaver.

</details>


### [175] [Evaluating Confidence-Gated Retrieval with Matched Trajectory Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26846)
*Prateek Chhikara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"匹配轨迹回放"协议来系统比较置信度到动作的映射，在六组模型-数据集组合上发现：事后等渗校准虽然能显著提高已承诺答案的准确率（最高提升41个百分点），但会降低覆盖率并增加检索使用；整体准确率在HotpotQA上最多提升15个百分点，在MuSiQue上却最多下降17个百分点。校准使模型更趋于选择性低风险操作点，但无法估计额外检索的预期收益，因此检索决策需要独立的"信息价值/效用"估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式语言模型代理使用置信度信号来决定立即回答、检索额外证据或推迟回答，但现有的置信度评估通常孤立进行，没有衡量其所触发动作在轨迹层面的后果。现有研究缺乏一个受控协议来公平比较不同置信度到动作的映射方式。

Method: 提出"匹配轨迹回放"（matched trajectory replay）受控协议，固定候选答案状态、证据点、预算和动作成本不变，在此框架下系统比较原始语言化置信度（raw verbalized confidence）与后验等渗校准（post-hoc isotonic calibration）在多跳问答系统中的表现，覆盖Mistral、GPT和Qwen三个模型以及HotpotQA和MuSiQue两个数据集。

Result: 在相同的数值承诺阈值下，校准改变了代理最终承诺回答的问题集合；在全部六组模型-数据集组合中，校准使已承诺答案的准确率最高提升41个百分点，但可能降低覆盖率并增加检索使用。整体准确率在HotpotQA上最高提升15个百分点，在MuSiQue上最多下降17个百分点。检索前拟合的校准映射在检索深度一和深度二上改善留出校准，但在深度三上劣于原始置信度。额外证据平均有帮助，但聚合效应不能说明置信度能识别哪条具体轨迹将从另一次检索中受益。

Conclusion: 校准可以解释承诺风险（使风险可解读），但无法估计另一次检索的预期收益。检索决策需要独立的"信息价值"或"效用"估计。评估报告应当包括留出校准、风险-覆盖率曲线和检索成本指标。

Abstract: Interactive language-model agents use confidence signals to decide whether to answer immediately, retrieve additional evidence (from memory or external knowledge), or defer. Yet confidence is usually evaluated in isolation, without measuring the trajectory-level consequences of the actions it triggers. We propose matched trajectory replay, a controlled protocol for comparing confidence-to-action mappings. The protocol holds candidate answer states, evidence points, budgets, and action costs fixed. We use it to compare raw verbalized confidence with post-hoc isotonic calibration in a multi-hop question-answering system using Mistral, GPT, and Qwen models on HotpotQA and MuSiQue datasets. At the same numerical commitment threshold, calibration changes which questions agents ultimately commit to answering. Across all six model-dataset pairs, it increases accuracy among committed answers by up to 41 percentage points. However, it can reduce coverage and increase retrieval use. Overall accuracy improves by up to 15 percentage points on HotpotQA but falls by up to 17 percentage points on MuSiQue. These effects reflect a shift to a more selective, lower-risk operating point, not improved answers or confidence ranking. A calibration map fitted before retrieval improves held-out calibration through retrieval depths one and two, but is worse than raw confidence at depth three for all three models. Additional evidence helps on average, but this aggregate effect does not establish whether confidence identifies which individual episodes will benefit from another retrieval. Taken together, these results show that calibration can make commitment risk interpretable, but it does not estimate the expected benefit of another retrieval. Retrieval therefore requires a separate value-of-information or utility estimate. Evaluations should report held-out calibration, risk-coverage, and retrieval cost.

</details>


### [176] [Planting a Latent Variable in Natural-Looking Text: a More Realistic Test of Belief States in LLMs and Their Link to Concept Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26887)
*Alexandru-Iulius Jerpelea*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过在自然文本中植入可控隐变量（通过稀疏自编码器方向进行"潜意识"引导），证明了小规模Transformer模型不仅能够追踪该隐变量的贝叶斯后验信念，还会在特征空间中按马尔可夫链的顺序将这8个状态排列成环形几何结构。这是首次将LLM的信念状态追踪能力与特征几何联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明LLM会追踪"信念状态"（对语言潜在变量的概率分布），但以往验证主要局限于合成玩具数据和个别案例，且从未在经验上将信念状态追踪与LLM特征的几何结构（可解释性研究所关注的概念空间排列）建立直接联系。

Method: 作者设计了一个受控实验：让LLM教师模型在生成自然文本时，以"潜意识"方式在每个token沿K=8个不相关的稀疏自动编码器（SAE）方向之一进行引导，这些激活方向遵循环形马尔可夫链的动态变化。然后在这样构建的语料上训练一个小型Transformer模型，检验其信念状态追踪能力和特征几何结构。

Result: 结果显示：(1) 小型Transformer确实能追踪所植入隐变量的贝叶斯后验信念；(2) 模型在特征空间中也将这8个状态排列成环形，且顺序与马尔可夫链的转移结构完全一致。这为"概念的几何结构由潜在变量的统计动态所塑造"提供了支持性证据。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在自然语言上下文上将信念状态追踪与特征几何建立实证联系，表明LLM对隐变量统计动态的内部表征会映射到激活空间中的概念几何排列，为理解LLM内部表征的结构来源提供了新视角。

Abstract: LLMs are thought to track "belief states," i.e., running probability distributions over the latent variables that govern language (Shai et al., 2024; Sarfati et al., 2026), but so far this has only been comprehensively demonstrated on toy synthetic data and in a few isolated case studies. It has also never been empirically connected to the geometry of LLM features (the concepts interpretability finds in model activations). In this work, we plant a controllable latent variable inside natural-looking text. An LLM teacher writes ordinary text while we "subliminally" steer it along one of K = 8 unrelated sparse autoencoder directions at each token, with the active directions following a ring-shaped Markov chain. A small transformer model trained on this corpus does indeed track the Bayesian posterior belief about our planted latent variable. Moreover, it also arranges the 8 states themselves on a ring, in the exact order of the Markov chain, which is supporting evidence that a concept's geometry can be formed by the statistical dynamics of the latent variable behind it.

</details>


### [177] [TabuLM: Morphology-Aware Tabular Pre-training for Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26923)
*Ireddi Rakshitha,Devavarapu Yashwanth,Ntakirutimana Pierre*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TabuLM，这是首个针对基尼亚卢旺达语（Kinyarwanda）表格数据进行预训练的语言模型。TabuLM在KinyaBERT-large基础上加入了行、列和单元格类型嵌入以及表格结构注意力偏置，并设计了掩码单元恢复（MCR）和列类型预测（CTP）两个新预训练目标。模型在172张卢旺达政府表格上预训练，并提出了首个基尼亚卢旺达语表格问答基准TabQA-kin，在526个问答对上取得了62.0%的精确匹配率，显著超越所有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基尼亚卢旺达语是一种形态丰富的班图语，拥有超过1200万使用者，但缺乏任何专门的表格表示学习资源。现有表格预训练模型和问答系统主要针对英语等主流语言，无法直接应用于低资源语言场景。因此，作者旨在填补这一空白，为该语言构建专用的表格语言模型。

Method: TabuLM在KinyaBERT-large（双层形态变换器）基础上进行扩展：（1）引入加性的行、列和单元格类型嵌入以编码表格结构信息；（2）设计可学习的表格结构注意力偏置，增强同行和同列单元之间的注意力；（3）提出两个新的预训练目标：掩码单元恢复（MCR）通过行和列上下文重建被掩码的完整单元格，列类型预测（CTP）根据观测单元格值预测列的语义类型。预训练数据来自NISR、RAB、REB和MoH四个卢旺达政府开放数据门户的172张表格（约35,000个单元格）。

Result: 在TabQA-kin基准上（526个问答对，覆盖31张表格和4种问题类型），TabuLM达到62.0%的精确匹配率，比KinyaBERT-large高出5.7个百分点，比mBERT（49.3%）和XLM-R（50.0%）分别高出11.7和12.0个百分点。分析表明，结构化的表格嵌入在比较类和查询类问题上最为关键，而形态学感知能力提供了互补性的增益。

Conclusion: TabuLM是首个针对基尼亚卢旺达语表格数据预训练的语言模型，验证了结合表格结构编码与形态学感知在低资源语言表格问答任务上的有效性。代码、数据和预训练权重全部公开，为该语言的后续研究和应用提供了基础资源。

Abstract: We present TabuLM, the first language model pre-trained on Kinyarwanda tabular data. Kinyarwanda is a morphologically rich Bantu language spoken by over 12 million people in Rwanda, yet lacks any dedicated tabular representation learning resource. TabuLM extends KinyaBERT-large, a two-tier morphological transformer, with additive row, column, and cell-type embeddings and a learned table-structure attention bias that sharpens same-row and same-column attention. Pre-training uses two new objectives: Masked Cell Recovery (MCR), which masks entire cells and forces reconstruction from row and column context, and Column Type Prediction (CTP), which predicts column semantic types from observed cell values. We pre-train on 172 Rwandan government tables (~35,000 cells) from NISR, RAB, REB, and MoH open-data portals, and introduce TabQA-kin, the first native Kinyarwanda table question-answering benchmark comprising 526 QA pairs across 31 tables and four question types. TabuLM achieves 62.0% exact match on TabQA-kin, outperforming KinyaBERT-large by 5.7 EM points and all multilingual baselines (mBERT 49.3%, XLM-R 50.0%) by 11.7-12.7 points. Analysis shows that structural table embeddings are most decisive for comparison and lookup questions, while morphological awareness provides complementary gains. Our code, data, and pre-trained checkpoint are publicly available.

</details>


### [178] [Mapping Written Words to Spoken Words in a Different Language Using Only Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26925)
*Gabriel Pirlogeanu,Dan Oneata,Horia Cucu,Herman Kamper*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对齐的方法，利用自监督语音表示将书面英语关键词映射到印地语口语词，无需转录或显式模型训练，在关键词识别和定位任务上优于之前的注意力神经模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低资源场景下，获取语音数据本身就困难。虽然让说话者描述图像是一种有前景的数据收集方式，但如何从视觉锚定的语音数据中构建关键词到口语词的跨语言映射仍是挑战。之前的工作依赖端到端多模态神经网络，作者希望探索更简单的对齐方法。

Method: 首先使用现成的图像字幕系统从图像中自动获取英语关键词标签。接着将与同一关键词关联的印地语话语使用自监督语音特征进行对齐，聚合对齐证据来识别对应目标词的重复语音片段。在对齐过程中还引入了负样本以提升效果。

Result: 在关键词识别（keyword spotting）和定位（localization）任务上，对齐方法优于之前的基于注意力的神经模型；加入负样例也能带来收益。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了无需转录或显式模型训练，仅依靠视觉锚定即可直接从数据中学习跨语言的词到语音映射，为低资源场景下的语音建模提供了更简单有效的替代方案。

Abstract: In many low-resource settings, even just eliciting speech for data collection is difficult. One promising approach has been to ask speakers to describe images. But how do we build models from such visually grounded speech data? Given a dataset of images with Hindi spoken captions, we consider how we can map a written English keyword to spoken realisations of that word in Hindi. Previous work trained end-to-end multimodal neural models. Instead, we explore a simpler alignment-based approach built on self-supervised speech representations. Written English tags are automatically obtained from images using off-the-shelf image captioning systems. Hindi utterances associated with the same keyword are then aligned (using self-supervised features), and alignment evidence is aggregated to identify recurring speech segments corresponding to the target word. Experiments evaluating keyword spotting and localization show that our alignment-based approach outperforms a previous attention-based neural model. We also show the benefit of incorporating negative examples during alignment. Our work demonstrates that cross-lingual word-to-speech mappings can be learned directly from visual grounding without transcriptions or explicit model training.

</details>


### [179] [KinyaEmbed: Contrastive Sentence Embeddings for Kinyarwanda via Multi-Stage Curriculum Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26941)
*Ireddi Rakshitha,Devavarapu Yashwanth,Ntakirutimana Pierre*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了KinyaEmbed，首个专为基尼亚卢旺达语（Kinyarwanda）设计的句子嵌入模型。通过四阶段课程训练（利用约18,000个官方公报释义对、715个NLLB翻译MNLI三元组、英语-基尼亚卢旺达语OPUS-100翻译对及2,936个KinyaCOMET过滤的高质量句子对），并引入新的无污染STS基准Wiki-RW-STS。七检查点集成在SemRel2024-rw上达到Spearman ρ=0.7298，超越mE5-large 20.9%和OpenAI text-embedding-3-large 41.0%，同时在文档聚类任务上取得最佳轮廓系数。所有资源公开可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基尼亚卢旺达语是一种形态丰富的班图语系语言，全球超过1200万人使用，但现有多语言嵌入模型（如LaBSE、mE5-large、OpenAI text-embedding-3-large）因其预训练语料中对该语言严重欠表示而表现不佳。因此需要一个专门为基尼亚卢旺达语设计的句子嵌入模型。

Method: 基于KinyaBERT-large构建，采用四阶段课程训练配合MultipleNegativesRankingLoss（MNRL）损失函数：阶段1使用约18,000对官方公报释义对和三种温度尺度；阶段2在715个NLLB翻译的MNLI三元组上微调以捕获蕴含结构；阶段3利用英语-基尼亚卢旺达语OPUS-100翻译对进行表示对齐；阶段4用2,936个经KinyaCOMET质量阈值0.8过滤的高质量句子对进行精调。最终采用七检查点集成策略（all5+23A*2，最后阶段双重加权）。同时构建了Wiki-RW-STS基准，包含300对从基尼亚卢旺达语维基百科中提取的无污染STS数据。

Result: KinyaEmbed在SemRel2024-rw上达到Spearman ρ=0.7298，超越mE5-large 20.9%，超越OpenAI text-embedding-3-large 41.0%；在文档聚类任务上取得最佳轮廓系数0.2146。在Wiki-RW-STS基准上的表现也被评估，所有检查点、过滤数据及基准均已公开。

Conclusion: KinyaEmbed证明了针对低资源语言的专用句子嵌入模型可以通过精心设计的课程训练策略和高质量数据过滤，显著超越通用多语言模型。该工作填补了基尼亚卢旺达语句子嵌入研究的空白，并为其他低资源语言提供了可复现的训练范式和公开基准。

Abstract: We present KinyaEmbed, the first dedicated sentence embedding model for Kinyarwanda, a morphologically rich Bantu language spoken by over 12 million people in Rwanda. Existing multilingual embedding models such as LaBSE, mE5-large, and OpenAI text-embedding-3-large perform poorly on Kinyarwanda due to severe under-representation in their pre-training corpora. KinyaEmbed is built on KinyaBERT-large and trained via a four-stage curriculum using MultipleNegativesRankingLoss (MNRL): Stage 1 leverages ~18,000 paraphrase pairs from the Official Gazette of Rwanda with three temperature scales; Stage 2 fine-tunes on 715 NLLB-translated MNLI triplets for entailment structure; Stage 3 aligns representations using English-Kinyarwanda OPUS-100 translation pairs; Stage 4 refines with 2,936 high-quality pairs filtered from KinyaCOMET at quality threshold 0.8. We evaluate on SemRel2024-rw and introduce Wiki-RW-STS, a new contamination-free Kinyarwanda STS benchmark of 300 pairs derived from Kinyarwanda Wikipedia. A seven-checkpoint ensemble (all5+23A*2, with the final stage double-weighted) achieves Spearman \r{ho}=0.7298 on SemRel2024-rw, surpassing mE5-large by 20.9% and OpenAI text-embedding-3-large by 41.0%. KinyaEmbed also achieves the best document clustering silhouette score (0.2146) across all evaluated models. All checkpoints, the KinyaCOMET filtered pairs, and the Wiki-RW-STS benchmark are publicly available.

</details>


### [180] [Squeezing More from Limited Data with Recursive Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26973)
*Serdar Gülbahar,Lukas Edman,Alexander Fraser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究有限数据预算下的预训练缩放规律，发现标准Transformer在小数据场景下因嵌入参数占比过高和计算与表征能力耦合而表现不佳，提出递归Transformer（共享块跨深度复用）结合分解嵌入来解决该问题，在10M-100M词预算下优于标准Transformer且与BabyLM Challenge 2025获奖结果竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定数据预算但计算充裕的有限数据场景下，标准Transformer的缩放规律与网页级语言建模不同：参数增加超过最优规模后会导致过拟合和泛化恶化。论文旨在研究这一行为，并解决标准Transformer在数据匮乏环境下扩展性差的问题——嵌入层消耗过多参数预算，且每token计算量与表征能力耦合。

Method: 在10M至100M词的预训练预算下，跨两个语料库和多个下游评测任务系统性地研究了最优模型规模与数据预算的关系。提出两类改进：(1) 递归Transformer，在同一可共享块上跨深度复用以增加计算深度；(2) 因子化嵌入，减少词汇表映射参数。

Result: 训练了三个递归模型，在10M和100M词预算下均优于标准Transformer，同时性能与BabyLM Challenge 2025的获奖模型保持竞争性。最优规模强烈依赖数据预算和下游目标任务。

Conclusion: 标准Transformer在小数据预训练场景下缩放表现差，递归架构与分解嵌入能更有效地解耦参数和计算，在受限数据预算下实现更好的泛化，提供了一种替代性的小规模预训练扩展方案。

Abstract: Pre-training under limited data requires a different view of scaling than web-scale language modeling. With a fixed data budget but relatively abundant compute, increasing parameter count helps only up to an optimal scale; beyond that point, models overfit and generalization worsens. We study this behavior across 10M-100M word pre-training budgets, two corpora, and multiple downstream evaluations, and find that optimal size depends strongly on both the data budget and the downstream target. We argue that standard Transformers scale down poorly to this setting, because embeddings consume a large fraction of the parameter budget and per-token computation is tied to representational capacity. To address this coupling, we study recursive Transformers, reusing a shared block across depth to scale compute, together with factorized embeddings to reduce vocabulary-map parameters. We train three recursive models and find that they outperform standard Transformers at 10M and 100M words, while remaining competitive with BabyLM Challenge 2025 winners.

</details>


### [181] [JudgeStealer: Extracting LLM Judging Capabilities across Evaluation Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26982)
*Chen Chen,Yaolin Chen,Xuehan Sun,Juan Lin,Xueluan Gong,Yuhang Zheng,Qian Wang,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出JUDGESTEALER，首个面向LLM裁判模型的查询高效模型提取框架，利用跨协议一致性将点式评分转换为成对和列表式监督信号，在有限查询预算下分别达到73.3%、87.0%和71.6%的准确率，并展现出对现有提取防御的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM裁判模型的判断能力具有高知识产权价值，但黑盒访问使其面临模型提取攻击的风险。现有提取方法未专门针对LLM裁判模型设计，且在受限查询预算下对多种评估协议（点式评分、成对比较、列表排序）的支持有限。因此需要一种查询高效的专用提取框架。

Method: JUDGESTEALER采用以下核心策略：(1) 利用跨协议强一致性，仅查询点式评分，再将其转换为成对和列表式监督信号，避免额外查询；(2) 动态选择点式输入，基于语义多样性、预测不确定性和潜在裁判偏差来捕获信息丰富的裁判模式；(3) 应用分数平滑和多协议审查，保留分数序结构并缓解代理模型适配中的灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在SOTA LLM-as-a-judge和奖励模型上的大量实验表明，JUDGESTEALER在点式、成对和列表式评估中分别达到73.3%、87.0%和71.6%的准确率，持续优于现有提取基线。该框架在不同代理模型规模、适配策略和推理设置下均保持有效，并对代表性提取防御展现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: JUDGESTEALER是首个查询高效的LLM裁判模型提取框架，通过跨协议信号转换和动态样本选择有效解决了多协议提取的挑战，证明了LLM裁判模型在面对模型提取攻击时的脆弱性，为后续防御研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) judges are increasingly used across various evaluation scenarios, making their judgment capabilities valuable intellectual property. However, black-box access exposes these capabilities to model extraction attacks. Existing extraction methods do not specifically target LLM judges and provide limited support for multiple evaluation protocols under restricted query budgets. In this study, we propose JUDGESTEALER, the first query-efficient model extraction framework for replicating judging capabilities across pointwise scoring, pairwise comparison, and listwise ranking protocols. JUDGESTEALER exploits the strong cross-protocol agreement to acquire pointwise scores and transform them into pairwise and listwise supervisions without additional victim queries. To capture informative judge patterns and improve query efficiency, JUDGESTEALER dynamically selects pointwise inputs based on semantic diversity, predictive uncertainty, and potential judge biases. It further applies score smoothing and multi-protocol review to preserve the ordinal structure of scores and mitigate catastrophic forgetting during surrogate adaptation. Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art LLM-as-a-judge and reward models show that JUDGESTEALER consistently outperforms existing extraction baselines, achieving up to 73.3%, 87.0%, and 71.6% accuracy for pointwise, pairwise, and listwise evaluation, respectively. JUDGESTEALER also remains effective across different sur- rogate model scales, adaptation strategies, and reasoning settings. Moreover, JUDGESTEALER demonstrates robustness against representative extraction defenses.

</details>


### [182] [ITL: Interpretable Document Alignment with Structured Reference Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27031)
*Raúl Giráldez,Dayrelis Mena,Jesús S. Aguilar--Ruiz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出智能目标定位器（ITL），一种领域无关、语言可移植的方法论，用于量化目标文档文本单元与结构化参考文档（SRD）中定义的概念之间的亲合度。ITL从SRD中归纳概念特异性术语档案（独立词、双词、三词及共现），并为每个术语赋予结合概念隶属度和区分度的权重，输出可在不同粒度聚合的文本单元-概念亲合矩阵。通过17项可持续发展目标（SDG）进行内部一致性评估，所有官方目标陈述均与对应概念达到最高亲合度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索和分类方法大多返回成对相似度分数或类别标签，缺乏能够在概念层面直接回溯到术语证据支持的评分方法。文档与结构化参考框架之间的对齐测量需要概念级、可解释且可追踪的量化指标，而现有方法难以同时满足这些需求。

Method: ITL从结构化参考文档（SRD）中归纳各概念专属的术语档案，涵盖独立术语、双词、三词及共现模式。每个术语分配权重，该权重综合了概念隶属度、术语类型特异性和概念间可区分性。最终输出文本单元-概念亲合矩阵，可在不同粒度级别进行聚合分析。

Result: 以17个SDG目标作为评估对象，每个官方目标陈述均与对应概念达到最高亲合度，且其余概念的均值亲合度相对于参考亲合度保持边缘水平。这种分离表明ITL能够有效区分框架中不同概念的特征轮廓。

Conclusion: ITL为量化文档与结构化框架的对齐程度提供了通用基础，同时保持了每个结果对支撑其术语证据的可追溯性，兼具可解释性和透明度。

Abstract: Measuring alignment between documents and structured reference frameworks requires identifying conceptual evidence distributed throughout the text and reporting it through measures that are quantitative, interpretable, and traceable. Many commonly used retrieval and classification approaches return either pairwise similarity scores or one or more class labels, whereas fewer methods provide concept-level scores that are directly traceable to the terminological evidence supporting them. We present \emph{Intelligent Target Locator} (ITL), a domain-agnostic and language-portable methodology that estimates the affinity between the textual units of a target document and the concepts defined in a \emph{Structured Reference Document} ($SRD$). From the $SRD$, ITL induces concept-specific terminological profiles built from independent terms, bigrams, trigrams, and co-occurrences. Each term is assigned an importance weight that combines concept membership, term-type specificity and inter-concept discriminability. The output is a textual-unit--concept affinity matrix that can be aggregated at different levels of granularity. We conduct an internal consistency assessment using the 17 Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs), evaluating each official goal statement against the $SRD$ induced from the same set of descriptors. Every statement reached its highest affinity with the corresponding concept, and the mean affinity across the remaining concepts stayed marginal relative to the mean reference affinity. This separation indicates that ITL distinguishes the conceptual profiles of the framework. ITL thus offers a general basis for quantifying document alignment with structured frameworks while keeping each result traceable to the terminological evidence that supports it.

</details>


### [183] [Representing and Parsing Korean Constituency Structure at Different Levels of Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27035)
*Jungyeul Park,KyungTae Lim,Zihao Huang,Eunkyul Leah Jo,Yige Chen,Chulwoo Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了韩语成分句法解析的表示挑战：由于韩语eojeol是形态复杂的空间单元，词组结构树的终端单元与表层词不完全对应。论文从Penn Korean Treebank构建了三种表示（Morpheme+XPOS、Eojeol+XPOS、Eojeol+UPOS），在统一建模和评估设置下，以gold词元切分和gold词类标签为条件，比较了三种非二叉转移式成分解析器（top-down、in-order、bottom-up）。结果表明morphologically richer的Morpheme+XPOS表现最强，Eojeol+XPOS次之，Eojeol+UPOS明显欠佳；而eojeol终端能产生更短的转移序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 韩语成分句法解析面临表示层面的根本挑战：现有成分句法资源在如何表示eojeol内部的形态信息和非显性元素方面存在差异，而这一表示层面的选择对解析性能的影响尚未被系统性比较。理解不同终端域（morpheme vs. eojeol）及不同词类粒度（XPOS vs. UPOS）对解析器的影响，对设计更优的韩语句法解析系统以及确定语言上合理的句法标注域具有重要价值。

Method: 从Penn Korean Treebank构造三种解析表示：Morpheme+XPOS、Eojeol+XPOS和Eojeol+UPOS。构造过程包括移除空元素、将Penn Korean短语结构与显性eojeol词元对齐、在可能处保留Penn Korean短语标签，并变化终端层和终端前层。在统一的建模和评估设置下，评估三种非二叉转移式成分解析器（top-down、in-order、bottom-up顺序），所有实验使用gold终端切分和gold终端前标签，因此条件于gold形态句法标注。此外，将Morpheme+XPOS的预测投影到eojeol终端域进行比较。

Result: Eojeol终端产生更短的转移序列（效率更高），但Eojeol+UPOS解析大幅低于形态信息更丰富的条件。Eojeol+XPOS缩小了这一差距，而Morpheme+XPOS在将其预测投影到eojeol终端域后仍给出最强结果。这表明在gold标注条件下，细粒度的形态和XPOS表示为所评估的解析器提供了有价值的证据。

Conclusion: 在gold形态句法标注条件下，细粒度形态信息和XPOS表示对解析器具有信息价值。然而，这一实证发现只涉及解析可用的信息，并不单独决定语言学上更优的终端域。从语言学和资源设计角度考量，eojeol是一个稳定且可解释的短语结构标注表层域，morpheme级和XPOS信息应作为对齐的形态句法证据保留。

Abstract: Korean constituency parsing raises a representational challenge because the terminal units of a phrase-structure tree do not straightforwardly correspond to simple surface words. Korean eojeols are morphologically complex spacing units, and existing constituency resources differ in how they represent eojeol-internal morphology and non-overt elements. This paper compares three constituency parsing representations derived from the Penn Korean Treebank: Morpheme+XPOS, Eojeol+XPOS, and Eojeol+UPOS. We construct these representations by removing null elements, aligning Penn Korean phrase structure with overt eojeol tokens, preserving Penn Korean phrase labels where possible, and varying the terminal and preterminal layers. We then evaluate canonical non-binary transition-based constituency parsers in top-down, in-order, and bottom-up orders under a shared modeling and evaluation setup. All experiments use gold terminal segmentation and gold preterminal labels and therefore evaluate constituency parsing conditioned on gold morphosyntactic annotation. Eojeol terminals yield shorter transition sequences, but Eojeol+UPOS parsing substantially underperforms the morphologically richer conditions. Eojeol+XPOS narrows this gap, while Morpheme+XPOS gives the strongest results even after its predictions are projected to the eojeol terminal domain. Under these gold-annotation conditions, the results show that fine-grained morphological and XPOS representations provide valuable evidence for the evaluated parsers. This empirical finding concerns the information available for parsing and does not by itself determine the linguistically preferable terminal domain. Independently, linguistic and resource-design considerations motivate eojeol as a stable and interpretable surface domain for phrase-structure annotation, with morpheme-level and XPOS information retained as aligned morphosyntactic evidence.

</details>


### [184] [Reasoning about In-Context Samples for Machine-Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27036)
*Maxime Bouthors,Josep Crego,François Yvon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于片段的推理框架，用于LLM机器翻译。模型先从检索到的相似示例中提取平行的源-目标片段，再将这些片段作为中间推理轨迹生成最终译文，并通过从大型教师模型中蒸馏片段和草稿来训练。在Qwen3模型家族、6种语言、每语言最多5个领域上的实验表明，该片段式翻译方法显著优于标准k-shot和基础草稿方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可以通过思维链推理提升响应可靠性，但如何将显式推理有效应用于基于上下文样本的LLM机器翻译仍不明确。作者希望探索一种适合翻译任务的推理框架，以提升翻译质量。

Method: 提出片段式推理框架：首先从检索到的相似示例中提取平行的源-目标片段，然后利用这些片段作为中间推理轨迹来生成最终翻译。训练阶段通过从大型教师模型中蒸馏银级片段和草稿来监督学生模型。

Result: 在Qwen3模型家族上，覆盖6种语言（每语言最多5个领域），片段式翻译显著优于标准k-shot和基础草稿方法。

Conclusion: 显式片断级推理可以有效提升LLM机器翻译的性能，片段式框架是优于传统k-shot和简单草稿方案的可行路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can be trained to perform chain-of-thoughts reasoning in order to improve the reliability of their responses. In this work, we investigate how explicit reasoning can be leveraged for LLM-Based Machine Translation (MT) with in-context samples. We introduce a novel fragment-based reasoning framework in which the model first extracts parallel source-target fragments from retrieved similar exemplars, and uses these fragments as intermediate reasoning traces to produce the final translation. To train our model, we distill silver fragments and drafts from a large teacher model. Our experiments with the Qwen3 model family, over 6 languages, including up to 5 domains per language, demonstrate that fragment-based MT significantly outperforms alternative methods like standard k-shot or basic drafting.

</details>


### [185] [Cascaded Batch Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27038)
*Sho Hoshino,Peinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出级联批量提示（cascaded batch prompting）方法，通过将复杂推理与符号接地解耦，解决传统批量提示在LLM推理中性能不可预测的问题，在多项选择问答和自然语言推理任务上超越单提示基线并实现与批量大小成正比的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批量提示虽能通过同时处理多个实例提升LLM推理效率，但存在下游任务性能不可预测的问题，需要一种既能保持效率又能保证稳定性能的方法。

Method: 提出两阶段的级联批量提示方法：第一阶段将复杂推理与符号接地（symbol grounding）进行解耦，第二阶段在解耦的基础上进行批量处理，从而消除传统批量提示的性能不确定性。

Result: 在多项选择问答（MCQA）和自然语言推理（NLI）任务上，该方法优于标准单提示基线，同时实现与批量大小成正比的推理加速，并在Pareto前沿上达到新的最优状态。

Conclusion: 级联批量提示有效解决了传统批量提示性能不可预测的缺陷，在保证推理准确性的同时实现了显著的效率提升，确立了其在效率-性能权衡（Pareto前沿）上的最优地位。

Abstract: Although batch prompting makes large language model inference more efficient by processing multiple instances simultaneously, it suffers from unpredictable downstream task performance. We propose cascaded batch prompting, a two-stage approach designed to resolve the unpredictability of conventional batch prompting by disentangling complex reasoning from symbol grounding. Experiments on multiple-choice question answering and natural language inference demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms the standard single prompting baseline while achieving a speedup proportional to batch size, establishing a new state of the art on the Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [186] [Research Design Tracking and Assessment for the Social Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27049)
*Marco Rovera,Sergiu Burlacu,Dominique Cappelletti,Alessio Tomelleri,Sonia Marzadro,Martina Bazzoli,Annalisa Tassi,Jessica Gagete-Miranda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了ARDTrA任务——自动检测社会科学论文中反事实研究设计并评估其应用质量。通过专家标注数据集和基于多轮RAG的对话式流程，发现段落长度是性能的主要驱动因素（解释52-66%的方差），且机器与人类在任务难度上存在独立差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会科学中因果研究设计的可靠评估对循证政策制定至关重要，但此前完全依赖人工专家分析，缺乏自动化的评估手段，亟需一个系统化的自动评估任务和方法。

Method: 设计了ARDTrA任务，覆盖六类反事实研究设计家族，构建专家标注数据集；采用基于多轮RAG（检索增强生成）的对话式流水线，组合四种检索策略、四种大语言模型和六种嵌入模型进行系统评估。

Result: 段落长度是性能的主要驱动因素，解释了52-66%的方差；按研究设计类型的分析显示，系统表现最困难的设设计并非专家标注分歧最大的设计，表明人与机器在任务难度上存在两个独立的来源。

Conclusion: ARDTrA为社会科学因果研究设计的自动评估提供了新基准，揭示了影响系统性能的关键因素（段落长度），并指出机器与人类专家在任务难度感知上的非对齐性，为未来研究设计评估的自动化发展指明了方向。

Abstract: Reliable assessment of causal research designs in the social sciences is critical for evidence-based policy-making, yet has so far relied entirely on manual expert analysis. We introduce Automated Research Design Tracking and Assessment (ARDTrA), a task that involves detecting the research design used in a paper and assessing the quality of its application. We create an expert-annotated dataset of papers covering six families of counterfactual research designs and evaluate the task using a multi-turn RAG-based conversational pipeline. Across four retrieval strategies, four LLMs and six embedding models, we find that passage length is the main driver of performance, explaining 52-66% of the variance. A per-research-design analysis also shows that human and machine difficulty do not align: the designs that prove hardest for the system are not those on which expert annotators disagree most, pointing to two independent sources of task difficulty.

</details>


### [187] [DocTalkBN: A Novel Dataset of Expert Telemedicine Conversations in Bengali](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27110)
*Anik Saha,Fahmida Sultana Naznin,Sadatul Islam Sadi,Ananya Shahrin Promi,Wahid Al Azad Navid,Rifat Shahriyar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DocTalkBN，一个大规模孟加拉语真实医患对话多模态数据集，包含557.63小时配对音频与文本、1,515次多轮患者通话、10,274个医患问答对，涵盖26个医学专科。基于此构建三个下游任务（医学分诊分类、建议安全性评估、医学命名实体识别）并基准测试多类大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的医疗对话AI需要真实的专家-患者交互数据，但此类数据集稀缺，尤其是孟加拉语等低资源语言。现有资源多源自医学论坛、书面健康内容或合成数据，缺乏真实对话的自发性、上下文丰富性和口语特征。

Method: 从全国播出的远程医疗节目中收集真实专家-患者对话，构建包含音频-文本配对的大规模多模态数据集DocTalkBN；在此基础上设计三个下游任务（医学分诊分类、建议安全性评估、医学NER），并基准测试多种大语言模型和基于编码器的基线模型。

Result: DocTalkBN被证明是实用性强的资源，尤其在临床推理任务上表现突出；数据集覆盖557.63小时音频文本、26个医学专科、1.7M tokens，并在多个下游任务上完成了基线基准测试。

Conclusion: DocTalkBN为低资源语言的可靠医疗NLP和更具文化适切性的医疗系统提供了宝贵资源，已公开源代码和数据集以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Reliable medical conversational AI requires authentic expert--patient interaction data, yet such datasets remain scarce, especially for low-resource languages such as Bengali. We present DocTalkBN, a large-scale multimodal dataset of real-world expert telemedicine conversations in Bengali, collected from nationally broadcast telemedicine programs featuring board-certified physicians. DocTalkBN contains 557.63 hours of paired audio and text, 1,515 multi-turn patient calls, 10,274 host--doctor question--answer exchanges, totaling 1.7M tokens, spanning 26 medical specialties. Unlike prior resources derived from medical forums, written health content, or synthetic data, our dataset preserves the spontaneity, contextual richness, and spoken characteristics of authentic medical interactions in a low-resource setting. To support benchmark-driven research, we further construct three downstream tasks from the corpus, medical triage classification, advice safety evaluation, and medical named entity recognition, and benchmark a diverse set of large language models and encoder-based baselines. Our results show that DocTalkBN is a practically useful resource, particularly for clinically grounded reasoning tasks. We release this resource to facilitate future research on reliable medical NLP and safer, more culturally grounded healthcare systems for low-resource languages. Our source codes and dataset are publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/doctalk.

</details>


### [188] [Cross-Lingual Alignment Without Joint Training: Do Monolingual Language Models Converge on Universal Representations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27115)
*Ej Zhou,Suchir Salhan,Catherine Arnett,Anna Korhonen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究纯单语语言模型（如Goldfish系列及不同实验室独立开发的模型）是否无需联合训练即可产生跨语言可对齐的表示。研究发现：这些模型在不同层间发展出可对齐的表示几何结构，且对齐程度随数据规模、模型规模或语言亲缘度增强而增强；单个Procrustes旋转即可将并行句子的隐藏状态在不同模型间映射；该旋转还能传递功能内容（如将旋转后的英文残差注入德文模型的实体完型任务可翻转预测结果）。这证明跨语言对齐可源于语言本身的结构与信息承载，而非联合训练，为模型拼接、合并及模块化多语言系统提供实践方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往跨语言对齐被视为多语言联合训练的产物（共享参数、混合语言批次或显式对齐目标）。本文质疑这一假设，探究在完全无联合训练的情况下，纯单语模型是否仍能自发产生可对齐的跨语言表示，从而理解对齐的本质来源并探索摆脱大规模多语言训练的可能性。

Method: 实验对象为严格的单语模型，包括Goldfish模型族和来自不同研究实验室的独立开发模型。方法包括：(1) 跨层分析表示几何的对齐程度，考察数据规模、模型规模和语言亲缘度的影响；(2) 在并行句子上拟合单个Procrustes旋转实现隐藏状态映射；(3) 通过将旋转后的英文残差注入德文模型的实体完型任务，验证旋转是否传递功能性内容（因果检验）。

Result: 三个核心发现：(1) 相关性——单语模型自发发展出可对齐的表示几何，且对齐随数据规模、模型规模或语言亲缘度增加而增强；(2) 构造性——单一Procrustes旋转即可在模型间映射隐藏状态；(3) 因果性——该旋转传递功能内容，在大多数情况下将英文旋转残差注入德文模型后可翻转事实完型预测至捐赠方（英文）的答案。

Conclusion: 跨语言对齐可以从语言本身的结构和信息承载中自发涌现，无需联合训练。该发现为模型拼接（stitching）、模型合并（merging）以及基于单语组件的模块化多语言系统构建提供了实际可行的未来方向。

Abstract: Cross-lingual alignment in multilingual language models is typically attributed to joint training: shared parameters, mixed-language batches, or explicit alignment objectives. We ask whether monolingual models trained on non-parallel data learn alignable representations without joint training. By testing on strictly monolingual language models, such as the Goldfish model families and independently developed models from different research labs, we find three results. Correlation: these models develop alignable representational geometry across layers, with alignment strengthening as data scale, model scale, or linguistic proximity increases. Construction: a single Procrustes rotation fit on parallel sentences maps hidden states between models. Causation: the same rotation transfers functional content; patching a rotated English residual into a German model on a factual cloze flips the prediction to the donor's capital in most cases. We confirm that cross-lingual alignment can emerge from the structure of language and the information it carries rather than from joint training, and this points to practical future directions including model stitching, merging, and modular multilingual systems built from monolingual components.

</details>


### [189] [TwinKV: A Composable Repair Pass for KV Cache Eviction via Pairwise Key Redundancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27128)
*Hong Chen,Yudong Zeng,Yongwei Huang,Zuhao Ouyang,Junyan Zhang,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TwinKV，一种无需训练、无需注意力机制的KV缓存冗余信号，通过检测键（key）是否存在近重复来识别可交换的token（孤儿与冗余捐赠者），在保持原有逐出策略预算和评分规则的前提下作为可组合的修复层，显著提升多种最新逐出策略在长上下文推理中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理受限于KV缓存的内存占用，现有逐出方法要么基于注意力分布评分，要么基于与全局参考点的距离，但本文通过受控留一法探针发现注意力大小与token对答案的因果贡献无关（Spearman ρ=-0.004），质疑了主流逐出方法的前提假设，因此需要一种与注意力无关、基于信息冗余的缓存压缩信号。

Method: TwinKV是一种无需训练、无需注意力的冗余信号，检测token的键在上下文中是否存在近重复副本。它不是替代现有逐出策略，而是作为可组合修复层：在给定策略固定保留集的情况下，识别已被逐出但无幸存副本的"孤儿"token，以及信息在其他地方重复的"冗余捐赠者"保留token，然后在保持原始预算和评分规则下交换二者。

Result: 在LongBench、LooGLE、RULER及MMLU-Pro短上下文无伤害对照上，以压缩比0.3/0.5/0.7评估四种近期逐出策略。Qwen3-4B上：两种策略多数配置有改进，一种近似持平，一种自适应基线已近性能上限仅少数配置受益；非上限策略在宽松压缩比下增益最小。RULER+Llama-3.2-1B上，第四种策略每个单元均有改进。Llama-3.2-1B在LongBench平均增益较小但改进单元比例更高，且在RULER上表现更干净。少样本分类示例是TwinKV无效的任务结构。

Conclusion: TwinKV作为一种与注意力无关、基于键冗余的修复层，能够在不改变逐出策略预算和评分规则的前提下提升多种KV缓存逐出策略的性能，但其提升效果依赖模型、任务及基线策略的类型，在已接近性能上限的自适应策略或少样本分类任务上增益有限。

Abstract: Long-context inference is bottlenecked by the memory footprint of the key-value (KV) cache, especially for small models under tight resource budgets. Existing KV cache eviction methods score tokens using the model's attention distribution or, in attention-free variants, each key's distance from a global reference point. Using a controlled leave-one-out probe, we find that attention magnitude is unrelated to a token's causal contribution to the answer (Spearman $ρ=-0.004$), challenging the premise behind dominant eviction methods. We introduce TwinKV, a training-free, attention-free redundancy signal that detects whether a token's key has a near-duplicate elsewhere in context. Rather than replacing existing policies, TwinKV acts as a composable repair pass: given a policy's fixed retained set, it identifies evicted tokens with no surviving duplicate (\emph{orphans}) and retained tokens whose information is duplicated elsewhere (\emph{redundant donors}), then swaps them while preserving the original budget and scoring rule. We compose TwinKV with four recent eviction policies across LongBench, LooGLE, RULER, and a short-context MMLU-Pro no-harm control at compression ratios ${0.3,0.5,0.7}$. On Qwen3-4B, TwinKV improves a majority of configurations for two policies, is near-even for a third, and helps only a minority for a fourth adaptive baseline already near a performance ceiling; gains across the three non-ceiling policies are smallest at the loosest ratio. On RULER with Llama-3.2-1B, however, that fourth policy improves in every evaluated cell because its Alone score leaves substantial room to improve. More broadly, Llama-3.2-1B shows a smaller average LongBench gain but a higher fraction of improved cells on LongBench and LooGLE than Qwen3-4B, plus a clean RULER win. We also identify few-shot classification exemplars as a task structure where TwinKV does not help on either model.

</details>


### [190] [Said Aloud, Read Different: Cross-Modal Instability in Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27135)
*Basel Mousi,Fahim Dalvi,Shammur Chowdhury,Firoj Alam,Nadir Durrani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个语音增强的视觉对比三元组基准，包含来自18个MENA国家的10,150张文化图像，用于评估多模态基础模型在语音vs文本、英语vs阿拉伯语条件下判断的一致性，发现模态和语言切换会引入显著的对比不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态基础模型越来越多用于语音优先的助手，需要解释语音查询并产生视觉决策，但语义等价的查询在不同模态（文本vs语音）和语言（英语vs阿拉伯语）下是否产生一致的判断尚不清楚。现有评估仅关注聚合准确率，无法捕捉推理碎片化等细粒度不一致。

Method: 构建语音增强的视觉对比三元组基准，包含10,150张来自18个MENA国家的文化图像，每张图像配对1个受支持陈述和2个看似合理但不受支持的备选陈述。定义对比不稳定性（contrastive instability）为模型未能解析三元组内全部陈述的条件比率，以区分碎片化推理与完全失败。在英语和阿拉伯语的文本及语音条件下评估多个多模态模型。

Result: 模态和语言的切换引入了显著的对比三元组级不一致性，而这种不一致性无法被聚合准确率完全捕获；语音输入会加剧部分失败（碎片化推理）。

Conclusion: 现有多模态基础模型在跨模态和跨语言的语义等价查询中缺乏稳健一致性，语音输入进一步放大了推理碎片化问题。基准已公开，为社区提供评估资源。

Abstract: Multimodal foundation models are increasingly used in speech-first assistants that must interpret spoken queries and produce visually grounded decisions. Yet it remains unclear whether semantically equivalent queries yield consistent judgments across modality (text vs. speech) and language (English vs. Arabic). We introduce a speech-augmented visually grounded contrastive triplet benchmark spanning 10,150 culturally grounded images from 18 MENA countries, where each image is paired with one supported statement and two plausible but unsupported alternatives. We define contrastive instability as the conditional rate at which a model fails to resolve all statements within a triplet, isolating fragmented reasoning from complete failure. Evaluating recent multimodal models under text and speech in English and Arabic, we find that modality and language shifts introduce substantial triplet-level inconsistencies that are not fully captured by aggregate accuracy, with speech amplifying partial failures. We make the benchmark publicly available to the community.

</details>


### [191] [STAR : Sentence Translation Alignment Rate for Document-to-Document Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27161)
*Yichen Dong,Hao Wang,Junhui Li,Linlong Xu,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出STAR指标用于量化文档级机器翻译中的句子级结构对齐度，并基于此提出StarPO框架，通过动态对齐掩码优化模型在未对齐片段上的表现，使紧凑模型在新闻和文学领域超越GPT-4o等大型专有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型推动了从句子级到文档级（Doc2Doc）机器翻译的转变，但单次文档生成常出现结构错位问题（句子遗漏或幻觉），违反源文与译文对应关系的核心要求，需要一种显式衡量句子级结构保真度的指标和相应的优化方法。

Method: 引入句子翻译对齐率（STAR）作为辅助指标来量化句子级结构保真度；在此基础上提出STAR掩码偏好优化（StarPO）框架，该框架按结构质量对文档级候选进行排序，并利用动态对齐掩码将优化聚焦于未对齐的片段。

Result: 在新闻和文学领域的实验结果表明，StarPO显著提升了翻译质量和结构完整性；紧凑模型借助StarPO可在性能上超越GPT-4o等大规模专有系统，同时保持更优的token效率。

Conclusion: StarPO通过结构感知的偏好优化有效解决了文档级翻译的结构错位问题，证明了紧凑模型在文档翻译任务上可以超越超大模型，为文档级机器翻译提供了兼顾质量与效率的可行路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have enabled a shift from sentence-level to document-to-document (Doc2Doc) machine translation, promising improved global coherence. However, document-to-document generation in a single pass frequently suffers from structural misalignment, manifesting as sentence omissions or hallucinations that violate the core requirement of source-target correspondence. To address this, we introduce Sentence Translation Alignment Rate (STAR), an auxiliary metric that explicitly quantifies sentence-level structural fidelity. Building on this, we propose STAR-masked Preference Optimization (StarPO), a framework that ranks document-level hypotheses by structural quality and utilizes a dynamic alignment mask to focus optimization on misaligned segments. Experimental results across news and literary domains demonstrate that StarPO significantly enhances translation quality and structural integrity. Notably, StarPO allows compact models to surpass the performance of massive proprietary systems like GPT-4o while maintaining superior token efficiency.

</details>


### [192] [Prediction of Prediction (PoP): Inter-Layer Activation Fusion for Single-Pass Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27165)
*Himal Badu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Prediction of Prediction (PoP)机制，通过融合自回归大语言模型单次前向传播中跨深度的中间隐藏状态转换动力学，在不额外生成或解码的情况下检测事实性错误，在TruthfulQA基准上达到75.5%的AUROC，仅增加不到1.2%的运行延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大语言模型常以高解码置信度生成事实错误输出，限制其在高风险场景中的部署。现有输出阶段的不确定性度量在模型对错误断言过度自信时会失效，而多样本验证管线则带来显著的显存和延迟开销。因此需要一种不依赖额外解码调用的内部信号来检测事实错误。

Method: PoP (Prediction of Prediction)机制通过捕获层间转换不确定性，在单次前向传播过程中融合跨深度的中间隐藏表示，以内部隐藏状态动力学特征作为事实正确性的分类信号。

Result: 在TruthfulQA基准上使用自回归transformer骨干网络，PoP在事实正确性分类任务上达到75.5%的AUROC；该机制在基础前向传播内运行，仅增加不到1.2%的运行延迟，且无需额外的生成轮次。

Conclusion: PoP证明仅靠单次前向传播中的内部隐藏状态转换动力学即可有效检测事实性错误，无需辅助解码调用或多样本验证，是一种低成本、高效的事实错误检测方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive large language models (LLMs) routinely generate factually incorrect outputs with high decoding confidence, limiting their deployment in high-stakes workflows. Existing output-stage uncertainty metrics can fail when models are overconfident on false assertions, while multi-sample verification pipelines introduce substantial memory and latency overhead. This work evaluates whether internal hidden-state transition dynamics during generation can signal factual errors without auxiliary decoding calls. We introduce Prediction of Prediction (PoP), a mechanism that captures layer-transition uncertainty by fusing intermediate hidden representations across depth during a single forward pass. Evaluated on the TruthfulQA benchmark using autoregressive transformer backbones, PoP achieves an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 75.5% for factual-correctness classification. The mechanism operates within the base forward pass, adding less than 1.2% runtime latency and requiring zero additional generation passes. The numerical results are reported from the author-verified experimental implementation and are bounded by the evaluation scope described below.

</details>


### [193] [When Text Misleads: Inconsistent-Aware Reasoning for Audio-Grounded Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27176)
*Yen-Ju Lu,Yuzhe Wang,Yaohan Guan,Xiluo He,Jiarui Hai,Mingrui Liang,Kaavya Chaparala,Thomas Thebaud,Laureano Moro-Velazquez,Najim Dehak,Jesus Villalba*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ContraTalk基准测试，系统性地识别和评估口语对话理解中的跨模态不一致问题（即模型仅依赖文本转录的捷径），并通过Audio Twin框架将声学线索显式暴露给推理模型，从而诊断和改进语音扎根推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有口语对话理解评估允许模型基于转录或单模态解决方案走捷径，无法判断模型是否真正从语音信号中扎根推理。作者将这种失败模式形式化为跨模态不一致（cross-modal disagreement），即转录暗示的表面解释与声学线索支持的答案相矛盾的情况，并希望创建可控基准来暴露和量化这一问题。

Method: (1) 提出可扩展框架，识别文本偏置的表面解释并将不一致区域转换为冲突问答样本，同时包含一致案例作为对照；(2) 构建ContraTalk基准，包含501个问题，覆盖五个话语维度（交互行为、情绪状态、对话行为、社会立场、对话意图）；(3) 开发Agent风格推理框架，将语音转换为Audio Twin——一种文本可读的局部声学线索表示，将声学证据暴露给推理模型。

Result: 纯文本LLM在一致案例中准确率超过90%，但在冲突案例中降至33-48%；直接音频LLM仅在部分程度上扎根，仍在30-40%的冲突案例中选择转录偏置陷阱；Audio Twin框架提升了冲突案例准确率并减少了陷阱选择，但一致案例行为仍依赖于骨干模型。

Conclusion: 转录捷径是口语对话理解中的重要失败模式；显式声学证据聚合（Audio Twin）提供了更可控的接口来诊断和改善语音扎根推理。

Abstract: Understanding spoken dialogue requires joint reasoning over lexical content and paralinguistic acoustic signals such as emotion and conversational intent. However, existing evaluations often allow shortcuts based on transcripts or single-modality solutions, obscuring whether models genuinely ground predictions in speech. We formalize this failure mode as cross-modal disagreement, where transcripts suggest plausible but incorrect surface interpretations while acoustic cues such as prosody or speaking style support different answers. We develop a scalable framework that identifies text-biased surface interpretations and converts disagreement regions into conflict QA examples. We also include consistent cases where transcript-based and speech-grounded interpretations agree, enabling evaluation beyond adversarial audio dependence. This results in ContraTalk, a controlled benchmark containing 501 questions across five discourse dimensions: interaction behavior, emotion state, dialogue act, social stance, and conversational intent. We further develop an agentic-style reasoning framework that converts speech into an Audio Twin, a text-readable representation of localized acoustic cues that exposes acoustic evidence to the reasoning model. Experiments show that strong text-only LLMs exceed 90% accuracy in consistent cases but drop to 33-48% in conflict cases. Direct AudioLLMs provide only partial grounding, still selecting the transcript-biased trap in roughly 30-40% of conflict cases. Our Audio Twin framework improves conflict-case accuracy while reducing trap selection, but its consistent-case behavior remains backbone-dependent. These results identify transcript-based shortcuts as an important failure mode in spoken dialogue understanding and show that explicit acoustic evidence aggregation provides a more controllable interface for diagnosing and improving speech-grounded reasoning.

</details>


### [194] [BALMS: Benchmarking Agentic LLMs for Longitudinal Mental Health Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27219)
*Yu Yvonne Wu,Arvind Pillai,Yuliang Chen,Yuwei Zhang,Sudarshan Regmi,Tess Z. Griffin,Michael V. Heinz,Lisa A. Marsch,Nicholas C. Jacobson,Andrew Campbell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了BALMS，这是首个针对LLM智能体系统在纵向心理健康感知上的系统化基准。BALMS覆盖3个真实纵向数据集、2类任务（封闭式幸福感评分预测和由LLM作为裁判自动评分的理由生成），并在5个开源和闭源LLM主干上评估3种智能体范式。研究发现零样本智能体很少能超越简单的均值基线，链式思维提示能改善推理型主干但无法保证时间接地性和数值正确性，突显了纵向心理健康智能体需要选择性检索历史、接地时间证据并在可解释行为特征上推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心理健康评估依赖偶发的自我报告量表，只能提供稀少的幸福感快照。可穿戴设备提供了纵向行为与生理信号，而现有的LLM驱动的个人健康智能体主要处理短期、基于检索的查询，并未评估智能体是否能对长期信号进行推理来预测幸福感评分并提供有证据支持的理由。这一空白促使作者构建BALMS基准。

Method: BALMS基准包含：3个真实世界纵向数据集，2类任务族（封闭式幸福感评分预测和由LLM-as-Judge自动评分的理由生成），在5种开源与闭源LLM主干上评估3种智能体范式。此外还进行了效率分析和时间尺度分析（temporal scaling）等额外分析。

Result: 零样本智能体很少能超越简单的均值基线，除非使用更强的主干或紧凑的、语义有意义的特征。链式思维提示能改善面向推理的主干，但无法保证时间接地性或数值正确性。论文还报告了效率和时间尺度方面的分析结果。

Conclusion: BALMS揭示了当前LLM智能体在纵向心理健康感知上的不足，强调了未来纵向心理健康智能体应具备三个关键能力：选择性检索历史信息、接地时间证据，以及在可解释行为特征上进行推理。

Abstract: Mental health assessment relies on episodic self-report scales, which convert subjective states such as stress into numerical scores but provide only sparse snapshots of wellbeing. Wearable devices offer longitudinal behavioral and physiological signals for continuous, low-burden monitoring. Recent LLM-driven personal-health agents enable natural language queries over wearable signals, but mainly handle short-term, retrieval-based lookups (e.g., highest step count over a week). They do not evaluate whether agents can reason over long-term signals to predict wellbeing scores paired with evidence-grounded rationales. To address this gap, we introduce BALMS, the first systematic benchmark of LLM-based agentic systems for longitudinal mental health sensing. BALMS spans 3 real-world longitudinal datasets, 2 task families (closed-form wellbeing-score prediction and rationale generation auto-graded by an LLM-as-Judge), 3 agentic paradigms evaluated across 5 open- and closed-source LLM backbones. We find that zero-shot agents rarely outperform a simple mean baseline, except with stronger backbones or compact, semantically meaningful features. Chain-of-thought prompting improves reasoning-oriented backbones, but does not guarantee temporal grounding or numerical correctness. Together with more analysis on efficiency and temporal scaling, BALMS highlights the need for longitudinal mental health agents that selectively retrieve history, ground temporal evidence, and reason over interpretable behavioral features.

</details>


### [195] [SCIT: Testing Causal Cache Carriers in Latent Chain-of-Thought Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27265)
*Yi Ding,Lijun Huang,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SCIT（Suffix Cache Interchange Test）是一种因果协议，用于识别链式思维模型中反事实计算由哪个Transformer对象承载。研究发现反事实算术主要经值缓存后缀轨迹迁移，而非隐藏状态或键；机制随模型能力呈现载体切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在链式思维模型将中间推理从可读文本转移到连续状态，虽提高了紧凑性，却掩盖了推理的因果载体。现有方法无法确定反事实计算实际由哪个Transformer组件（隐藏状态、键、值缓存等）承载，需要一种严格的因果协议来定位计算载体。

Method: SCIT协议包括：构造精确的源-接收者反事实；对声明的缓存段执行补丁操作；结合充分性检验与K/V组件拆分、隐状态对照、语义源对照、解码验证和匹配损坏。通过对CODI-GPT2和Sim-CoT风格GPT-2复现模型进行系统测试，并扩展到不同规模（1B/8B）的模型进行载体机制图谱绘制。

Result: 反事实算术主要通过值缓存后缀轨迹转移，而非隐藏状态、键、可重用答案槽或单token触发器。CODI-GPT2主检查点获得充分的充分性和必要性证据；Sim-CoT风格检查点显示相同的充分性和解码控制模式，但匹配损坏证据不足以做出必要性判定。非算术GPT-2/1B细胞保留潜在尾部值/KV传输，而8B和修复的非算术细胞通过提示前缀或全缓存K/V路由。

Conclusion: SCIT提供了一种缓存级诊断工具，揭示了检查点特异的GPT-2算术机制，并提出了一种能力门控的载体图谱，修正了"潜在尾部携带所有关键推理"的普适主张，表明计算载体随模型能力动态切换。

Abstract: Latent chain-of-thought models move intermediate reasoning from emitted text into continuous states, improving compactness but hiding the causal object. We introduce SCIT, the Suffix Cache Interchange Test, a causal protocol that constructs exact source-recipient counterfactuals, patches declared cache segments, and identifies which transformer object carries the counterfactual computation. SCIT combines sufficiency tests with K/V component splits, hidden-state controls, semantic source controls, decoded validation, and matched corruption. On CODI-GPT2 and a Sim-CoT-style GPT-2 reproduction, counterfactual arithmetic transfers primarily through value-cache suffix trajectories rather than hidden states, keys, reusable answer slots, or single-token triggers. Complete sufficiency-and-necessity evidence for the late-value-suffix mechanism holds for the main CODI-GPT2 checkpoint; the Sim-CoT-style checkpoint shows the same sufficiency and decoded-control pattern but insufficient matched-corruption evidence for a necessity call. Beyond these local arithmetic cells, SCIT reveals carrier-regime shifts: arithmetic-like GPT-2/1B cells preserve latent-tail value/KV transfer, whereas competent 8B and repaired non-arithmetic cells route through prompt-prefix or full-cache K/V; boundary cells receive no mechanism call. SCIT therefore contributes a cache-level diagnostic, a checkpoint-specific GPT-2 arithmetic mechanism, and a competence-gated carrier map rather than a universal latent-tail claim.

</details>


### [196] [Difference-in-Differences on a Censored Rating Scale Can Manufacture an Effect: Evidence from a Pre-Registered LLM-Judge Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27309)
*Shuyi Fan,Boyuan Deng,Mengyu Xu,Xinhong Xie,Chenyang Li,Hongyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出，通过双重差分（double difference）比较匹配条件下LLM评判者偏见的审计方法，其终点指标在报告所用的有界评分量表上并非可识别的。由于每项差分量都受到自身边界截尾（censoring）的影响，观测到的统计量混淆了"真实偏好差异"与"由于不对称截尾造成的差异衰减"。作者在预注册的冻结教学评判者审计中实证展示了这一失败：名义上显著的交互效应+0.378（p=0.002）实际上并非偏好信号，仅由严重性偏移（severity shift）和量表下限即可复制79%~85%的效应量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评判者的审计常常采用双重差分设计来认证偏见，但作者发现这种设计的核心统计量在有界评分量表上存在根本的识别问题——观测到的交互效应可能纯粹由截尾不对称产生，而非真实的偏好差异，这威胁到大量审计结论的有效性。

Method: 作者通过理论推导（closed-form）证明双重差分每项都受各自的截尾比例影响，导致严重性偏移在不平等截尾条件下制造虚假交互效应。随后在一项预注册的、对冻结教学评判者（990次API调用，封存于调用前）的审计中实证验证：将主效应设为被陈述学习者档案对支架偏好影响的效应量，并检查交互效应，然后用一个不包含任何差分偏好的人造构造复现交互效应。

Result: 注册的主要终点（学习者档案对评判者支架偏好的效应）为无效结果：+0.085分（95% BCa [-0.167, +0.353], p = 0.684）。审计中唯一的显著交互效应+0.378（p = 0.002）无法被识别为偏好：一个不含任何差分偏好的构造仅从观测到的严重性偏移和量表下限就能复制79%~85%的该交互效应。

Conclusion: 双重差分审计设计在有界量表上的终点指标存在系统性识别缺陷：观测到的交互效应混淆了差分偏好与差分截尾衰减，且该混淆可量化为可测的自审计内部的贡献。这意味着该类审计的主要统计量被严重性偏移所污染，即使主效应不显著，虚假的交互效应也可能被误读为偏见证据。

Abstract: Audits of LLM judges certify a bias by contrasting matched conditions, and the strongest designs difference twice: a within-item contrast between two candidate responses, differenced again across a manipulated attribute, read off a bounded rating scale. We show that this endpoint is not identified on the scale that reports it. Each term of the double difference is censored by its own share, so the observed statistic confounds differential preference with differential attenuation: a severity shift common to both responses manufactures an interaction whenever the two censor it unequally, as unequal distances from the bounds make them, exactly where good stimuli place them. We exhibit the failure inside a pre-registered audit of a frozen pedagogy judge, sealed before the first of its 990 calls. The registered primary endpoint, the effect of a stated learner profile on the judge's scaffolding preference, is null: $+0.085$ points (95\% BCa $[-0.167, +0.353]$, $p = 0.684$). The audit's one nominally significant interaction, $+0.378$ ($p = 0.002$), is not identified as preference: a construction containing zero differential preference reproduces 79 to 85\% of it from the observed severity shift and the scale floor alone. We derive the mechanism in closed form and show that its contribution is measurable from an audit's own ratings.

</details>


### [197] [BTS-AgentBench: A Deterministic, Replayable Pipeline from Read-Only Telemetry Logs to Agent Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27334)
*Jeong-Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种遥测数据到智能体任务的构建方法BTS-AgentBench，将工业站点的只读遥测记录编译为可执行的多轮智能体任务，发布532行数据集并在多个维度上做了质量保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业站点包含大量只读遥测数据，但缺乏将这些记录编译为可执行多轮智能体任务的基准，现有基准未能覆盖这一场景。

Method: 提出遥测到episode的构建流水线：将BTS元数据和原始历史标准化为只读工具库，使用工具派生金标准和证据编译静态任务，并将保留任务提升为类型化、有界、面向操作员的episode。通过代码契约预检、构建排除控制器（0/532行完成）、双重独立构建交叉验证（11个逻辑工具库导出完全匹配），并在XAI4HEAT上复现204个episode。

Result: 发布532行数据集（train/dev/test为356/87/89），包含澄清、目标修订、时间戳策略、质量门控报告和证据归因等功能；契约预检零发现，控制器完成0/532行；两次独立构建复现了全部11个工具库导出及分割结果；在XAI4HEAT上生成204个episode，其41行测试分割上GPT-5.5全部完成。

Conclusion: BTS-AgentBench提供了一套可复制、可验证的遥测到智能体任务构建方法，保证了数据构建的可信性与泛化能力，代码和产物已公开。

Abstract: Industrial sites contain large volumes of read-only telemetry, but few benchmarks specify how to compile these records into executable multi-turn agent tasks. We present a telemetry-to-episode construction method instantiated as BTS-AgentBench. The pipeline normalizes BTS metadata and raw histories into a read-only tool store, compiles static tasks with tool-derived gold answers and evidence, and lifts retained tasks into typed, bounded operator-facing episodes. The 532-row release adds clarification, goal revision, timestamp policy, quality-gated reporting, and evidence attribution while preserving the source computation and split. Coded contract preflight reports zero findings, and the construction-exclusion controller completes 0/532 rows. Two independent raw-to-episode builds match all 11 logical tool-store exports and reproduce the released 356/87/89 train/dev/test artifact exactly. Applying the shared construction path to XAI4HEAT produces 204 episodes; on its 41-row held-out test split, the controller completes 0 rows and the retained GPT-5.5 execution completes all 41. Code, artifacts, and replay reports are available at https://github.com/kjy7567/BTS-AgentBench.

</details>


### [198] [Pair-Level Essay-Scale Republication and Reuse from Fragmented Historical Text Reuse: A Workflow Study on Eighteenth-Century Books and Newspapers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27343)
*Ke Shu,Kira Hinderks,Eetu Mäkelä,Mikko Tolonen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究从碎片化文本复用证据中恢复长文（essay-scale）的再版与复用关系，以休谟论文在ECCO书籍与历史报纸中的候选对为对象，将任务形式化为配对层面的证据整合。比较了规则工作流、基线（决策树与直接LLM）及自动规则适配三类方法，结果表明配对层特征聚合可达0.948 F1，最终工作流在精度-召回权衡上最优，LLM基线更适合作为高召回候选扩展器，而人工审计确认了所有176个预测正例均为真实复用案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文级再版与复用的恢复不能仅依赖碎片检索，核心挑战在于将分散的文本复用证据在配对层面进行整合。由于正例覆盖天然不完整且输入仅为碎片化命中而非干净的文档对，需要一种结构化的证据合并方法，以生成紧凑且可审计的候选空间供历史研究检验。

Method: 将任务形式化为配对级证据整合（pair-level evidence consolidation），在三类方法族间进行比较：(1) 分阶段的基于规则的工作流（staged rule-based workflow）；(2) 基线方法，包括决策树和两种直接LLM提示设置；(3) 自动规则适配（automated rule adaptation）。此外还进行了特征聚合并应用于ECCO-ECCO切片与ECCO-Newspaper全量候选空间。

Result: 在标注的ECCO-ECCO切片上，单纯配对级特征聚合已达到0.948 F1；最终工作流在规则各阶段中给出最强精度-召回权衡。在全量ECCO-ECCO候选空间中，直接LLM基线标记多达14,886对为再版（最终工作流仅771对），表明其在该直接提示设置下充当高召回候选扩展器而非受精度控制的部署分类器。在ECCO-Newspaper上，人工审计确认全部176个预测正例为真实的再版或复用案例。

Conclusion: 在不完整金标准条件下，可审计的配对级证据整合是生成紧凑候选空间供历史检查的实用方案。论文还揭示了报纸期刊的问题重复与来源侧多重性中包含额外的溯源结构信息。

Abstract: This paper addresses the recovery of essay-scale republication and reuse from fragmented text-reuse evidence, a setting whose central challenge is pair-level evidence consolidation and not fragment retrieval alone. The study focuses on a candidate set centered on essays by eighteenth-century Scottish philosopher David Hume, spanning books from ECCO (Eighteenth Century Collections Online) and historical newspapers. Because the input consists of fragmented reuse hits instead of clean document pairs, and positive coverage is inherently incomplete, we formulate the task as pair-level evidence consolidation into plausible transmission relations and compare three methodological families: a staged rule-based workflow, baselines (a decision tree and two direct LLM settings), and automated rule adaptation. On labeled ECCO--ECCO slices, pair-level feature aggregation alone already reaches 0.948 F1 on the main labeled slice, while the final workflow gives the strongest overall precision-recall trade-off among the tested rule stages. On the full ECCO--ECCO candidate universe, direct LLM baselines flag up to 14,886 pairs as reprints compared to 771 for the final workflow, behaving in this direct-prompt setup as high-recall candidate expanders rather than precision-controlled deployment classifiers. On ECCO--Newspaper, manual audit confirms all 176 predicted positives as genuine cases of republication or reuse, while issue duplication and source-side multiplicity reveal additional provenance structure. Under incomplete ground truth, auditable pair-level evidence consolidation provides a practical way to produce compact candidate spaces for historical inspection.

</details>


### [199] [INTENT-AS-A-TOOL Makes it Easy to Track Agentic Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27348)
*Yutong Zhang,Jianshuo Dong,Peng Xu,Long Wang,Jie Zhang,Tianwei Zhang,Xiaoping Zhang,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出INTENT-AS-A-TOOL方法，通过为LLM智能体添加意图定向工具，使模型在推理过程中拥有表达目标行为承诺的专门通道，从而提供无需评判器的细粒度意图信号，用于在线监测和干预智能体的有害行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为自主智能体部署时，安全失败往往涉及实际后果性行动。现有CoT监控虽然能发现有害执行前的意图信号，但事后CoT标签过于粗糙，无法展示意图在生成过程中的动态变化，难以支持在线干预。

Method: 引入INTENT-AS-A-TOOL方法，为模型添加意图定向的工具接口，使模型可以通过调用该工具来表达对特定目标行为的承诺。通过计算意图工具被调用的概率，得到无需评判器的细粒度意图信号，从而将事后CoT标签扩展为密集的意图轨迹。

Result: 实验结果表明，INTENT-AS-A-TOOL能够补充CoT监控的不足，将事后CoT标签细化为密集的意图轨迹，并能识别出可用于在线干预的关键步骤。

Conclusion: 动作偏好（action preferences）可以作为追踪智能体在推理过程中错位（agentic misalignment）的有效信号，为在线安全干预提供新的技术路径。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are deployed as autonomous agents, safety failures increasingly involve consequential actions. We study agentic misalignment, where agents take harmful actions under goal conflicts and pressures. Using chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring, we find that harmful execution is often preceded by intent signals in reasoning. However, post-hoc CoT labels are too coarse to show how intent changes during generation. We introduce INTENT-AS-A-TOOL, an approach that adds intent-targeted tools to give the model a dedicated channel for expressing commitment to a target behavior. The probability of calling an intent tool provides a judge-free, fine-grained signal of the model's tendency to pursue that behavior. Our results show that INTENT-AS-A-TOOL complements CoT monitoring, expands post-hoc CoT labels into dense trajectories, and identifies critical steps for online intervention. These findings suggest that action preferences are useful for tracking agentic misalignment during reasoning. Our code and data are accessible: https://github.com/RebeccaZhang22/intent-as-a-tool.

</details>


### [200] [RCMN: Understanding Misleadingness in Influential Public Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27358)
*Peiling Yi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出"读者中心误导性理解"（RCMN）框架，从五个维度（误导机制、读者可能解读、证据支持的解读、情绪唤起、传播意图）系统化刻画公众话语中的误导性，构建基于证据的数据集，并评估轻量级表征在误导性分析中的效力，发现读者层面的解读可由轻量表征恢复，但误导机制的理解仍需更丰富的上下文化和证据支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有影响力的公共话语虽然能塑造公众信念，但也可能通过信息的框架、省略、情境化和传播方式造成误导——而不仅是陈述本身。然而现有研究对"误导性如何产生"以及"如何影响读者解读"关注不足，缺乏系统化的操作性框架。

Method: 提出RCMN框架，将误导性操作化为五个维度：误导机制、读者可能解读、证据支持的解读、情绪唤起和传播意图。基于该框架构建以证据为依据的有影响力公共话语数据集，并进行实证分析与跨五个生成式基础模型的评估。

Result: 实证发现误导性具有多样性，远超捏造范畴，无依据推断、夸大和省略是常见机制，且常伴随高情绪唤起和扭曲的传播意图。轻量级声明-上下文表征可恢复读者层面的解读，但识别误导机制仍然困难。

Conclusion: 轻量级表征对可扩展的误导性分析具有潜力，但可靠理解误导机制仍需更丰富的上下文化和证据支撑。

Abstract: Influential public discourse shapes public beliefs and can also mislead, not only through what is stated, but also through how information is framed, omitted, contextualised, and communicated. Yet less research has focused on how such misleadingness arises and shapes the interpretations formed by readers. To address this gap, we introduce Reader-Centric Misleadingness Understanding (RCMN), a framework that operationalises misleadingness through five dimensions: misleading mechanism, likely reader interpretation, evidence-warranted interpretation, emotional arousal, and communicative intent. Based on this framework, we construct an evidence-grounded dataset of influential public discourse. Empirical findings show that misleadingness is diverse and extends well beyond fabrication, with unsupported inference, exaggeration, and omission among the prevalent mechanisms, and is frequently associated with heightened emotional arousal and distortive communicative intent. Moreover, we investigate whether lightweight claim-and-context representations retain sufficient cues for understanding reader-centric misleadingness without access to richer contextual, evidential, and multimodal information. Evaluation across five recent generative foundation models shows that reader-level interpretations can often be recovered from such limited representations, whereas identifying how misleadingness is produced remains considerably more challenging. These findings highlight the potential of lightweight representations for scalable misleadingness analysis, while reliable understanding of misleading mechanisms continues to require richer contextual and evidential grounding.

</details>


### [201] [Your Voice Cloning System is Secretly a Voice Anonymizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27360)
*Romolo Muletta,Felix Matthias Saaro,Mark Cieliebak,Jan Deriu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用多语言语音克隆模型XTTSv2（基于27k小时语音训练）进行说话人匿名化，无需重新训练。通过伪说话人条件实现语音转换，并引入迭代优化策略平衡隐私与可用性。在七种欧洲语言上达到接近最优的隐私水平（EER≈0.49），同时保持竞争性的可懂度和更优的语音质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 说话人匿名化旨在从语音中抑制说话人身份特征，同时保留语言内容和语音质量。现有方法通常需要大量语言特定训练数据，泛化能力有限。作者希望利用已有的多语言语音克隆模型，在不重新训练的前提下实现高效、跨语言的说话人匿名化。

Method: 核心创新在于利用XTTSv2语音克隆模型中"韵律结构独立于说话人身份"的特性，通过条件化伪说话人实现语音转换。具体包括：1) 复用XTTSv2的语音克隆能力进行说话人转换；2) 提出迭代精炼策略，通过最大化说话人相异度与可懂度的调和平均值来平衡隐私和可用性；3) 无需任何语言特定训练，直接适配多语言场景。

Result: 在CommonVoice和Multilingual LibriSpeech数据集上，覆盖七种欧洲语言进行评测。系统实现了接近最优的隐私性能（EER≈0.49），具备竞争性的可懂度，并且在语音质量上显著优于专门的匿名化基线系统，同时无需语言特定训练。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，现有的多语言语音克隆模型可以零训练迁移到说话人匿名化任务中，实现隐私、可懂度和语音质量三者间良好的权衡。这一思路为跨语言说话人匿名化提供了新的范式，降低了训练成本并提升了泛化能力。

Abstract: Speaker anonymization suppresses speaker-identifying attributes from speech while preserving linguistic content and quality. We propose repurposing XTTSv2, a multilingual voice cloning model trained on 27k hours of speech, for speaker anonymization without retraining. Our key insight is that XTTSv2's voice cloning capabilities preserve prosodic structure independently of speaker identity, enabling voice conversion by conditioning on a pseudo-speaker. We introduce an iterative refinement strategy that balances privacy and utility by maximizing a harmonic mean of speaker dissimilarity and intelligibility. Evaluated on seven European languages across CommonVoice and Multilingual LibriSpeech, our system achieves near-optimal privacy (EER $\approx$ 0.49), competitive intelligibility, and substantially better speech quality than dedicated anonymization baselines, while requiring no language-specific training. We release the code here: https://github.com/rm00cr/coqui-tts.

</details>


### [202] [Puro-2B: Poor Lab's Qwen2-1.5B Trained on RTX 5090 within $5090](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27370)
*Kairong Luo,Jiarui Cui,Yaorui Yin,Shengqi Chen,Yiming Yang,Linxiang Gao,Yanmohan Wang,Mingzhe Zhang,Kaiyue Wen,Kaifeng Lyu,Wenguang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套低成本、开放源码的预训练配方（Puro-2B），在消费级RTX 5090 GPU上使用FP8精度从头训练2B参数模型，最高1.4万亿token，最佳训练成本不到$6.9K即可接近Qwen2.5-1.5B的性能。同时提出了Puro成本缩放定律，并提供了数据课程对下游性能影响的案例研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型预训练成本极其高昂，使得学术界和开源社区难以参与。虽然已有开源权重和训练配方，但缺少成本高效、硬件可及且完全开源的预训练方案。例如训练Llama-3.2-3B需花费超过$150万美元，复现SmolLM3-3B也需要超过$70万美元，这严重阻碍了开源社区的研究与创新。

Method: 作者提出了一套完整的开源预训练配方，核心包括：(1) 硬件选择：使用消费级RTX 5090 GPU大幅降低硬件门槛；(2) 低精度训练：采用FP8精度减少计算和存储开销；(3) 超球优化（hyperball optimization）提高训练效率；(4) 课程模型平均（curriculum model averaging）策略；(5) 精心设计的数据配方。基于此训练了包含不同token预算和配方变体的Puro-2B模型集合（最高1.4万亿token），并拟合了训练成本与平均性能之间的Puro成本缩放定律。

Result: 最佳模型在不到$6.9K的计算成本下训练，性能接近Qwen2.5-1.5B。Puro成本缩放定律表明约$4.4K（低于$5,090）即可达到Qwen2-1.5B的性能水平，成本较传统方案降低了两个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 通过完整的开源预训练配方，预训练成本可被大幅降低，使学术界和开源社区能够以极低成本（数千美元级别）参与大规模语言模型预训练研究。此外，完整的预训练管线（而非仅有模型权重）使得对数据课程等问题的受控研究成为可能。所有数据、代码和模型权重均以Apache 2.0协议开源发布。

Abstract: Language model pretraining has become almost synonymous with prohibitive cost, placing it out of reach for much of the academic and open-source communities. Although strong open-source efforts already exist, including open-weight models and open-source training recipes, a cost-efficient, hardware-accessible, and open-source pretraining recipe has long been missing. Even at a small scale, training Llama-3.2-3B costs over \$1.5M, and reproducing SmolLM3-3B needs over \$700K. In this report, we present an open pretraining recipe designed to lower this barrier. Using this recipe, we train a collection of Puro-2B models from scratch on up to 1.4 trillion tokens with FP8 precision on consumer-grade RTX 5090 GPUs. The models in the collection differ in token budgets and selected recipe variants. Our best model is trained at a compute cost of less than \$6.9K and approaches Qwen2.5-1.5B performance under our evaluation protocol. This cost efficiency is enabled by a combination of approaches, including hardware selection, low-precision training, hyperball optimization, curriculum model averaging, and the data recipe. Beyond the recipe itself, we provide two additional results. First, across the Puro-2B collection, we derive a Puro Cost Scaling Law that relates training cost to average model performance; the fitted law suggests that about \$4.4K, less than \$5,090, is sufficient to reach the performance of Qwen2-1.5B. Second, as an end-to-end case study, we examine how pretraining data curricula shape downstream performance after post-training. Such controlled studies are enabled by having access to the full pretraining pipeline rather than model weights alone. We release the full training recipe for Puro-2B, including data, code, and model weights under Apache 2.0 at https://huggingface.co/collections/thu-pacman/puro-2b.

</details>


### [203] [D2C-Routing: Dimension-to-Composition Evidence Routing for Mixed-Origin AI-Generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27380)
*Xin Chen,Fuwei Zhang,Yiqi Tong,Wei Guo,Yutian Xiao,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出D2C-Routing方法，将混合来源文本检测从二元文档级判断转化为维度到构成（dimension-to-composition）的来源归因问题，先推断内容来源和表达来源，再组合为四种协作类型。在MixD2C基准上达到0.8603四路平均TPR@1%FPR，比同划分的RACE-local重跑高6.5个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成文本检测通常被框架化为二元文档级判断（人类写作 vs. 机器生成），但这一框架在混合来源写作场景下失效，因为内容来源（content origin）和表达来源（expression origin）可能不一致。

Method: 将混合来源检测重新定义为维度到构成的来源归因问题，先分别推断内容来源和表达来源，再将其组合为四种协作类型。提出D2C-Routing（Dimension-to-Composition Routing），将内容侧和表达侧的证据路由到有监督的维度头，再通过一个可学习的门控组合层预测最终标签。

Result: 在MixD2C数据集（由HART混合来源基准重建的划分）上，提出的D2C-Routing检测系统达到0.8603四路平均TPR@1%FPR，比同划分的RACE-local重跑高出6.5个百分点。核心消融实验支持路由设计，错误分析表明区分AI内容/人类表达与完全AI生成文本仍是最难界定的边界。

Conclusion: D2C-Routing在混合来源文本检测中表现优于基线，验证了将检测解耦为内容维度和表达维度分别归因再组合的有效性。混合来源检测仍存在难点，尤其是AI内容与人类表达的组合类型与完全AI生成文本之间的区分。

Abstract: AI-generated text detection is commonly framed as a binary document-level judgment about whether a text is human-written or machine-generated. This framing breaks down for mixed-origin writing, where content origin and expression origin may differ. We cast mixed-origin detection as dimension-to-composition source attribution, inferring content origin and expression origin before composing them into four collaboration types. We propose Dimension-to-Composition Routing (D2C-Routing), which routes content-side and expression-side evidence to supervised dimension heads before a learned gated composition layer predicts the final label. On MixD2C, a reconstructed split derived from the HART mixed-origin benchmark, our disclosed D2C-Routing-based detector system reaches 0.8603 four-way Avg TPR@1%FPR, 6.5 points above the same-split RACE-local rerun. Core ablations support the routing design, while error analysis shows that distinguishing AI-content/human-expression from fully AI-generated text remains the hardest boundary. Code is available at https://github.com/bystander563/d2c-routing-artifact.

</details>


### [204] [RATIO: A Benchmark for Retrieval Across Typed Ideation Operations in Scientific Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27394)
*Maayan Sharon,Tom Hope*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RATIO，一个大规模的文献检索基准，将科学文献检索中的"灵感"操作定义为三种语义移动（Address、Broaden、Specify），并基于全文本论文语料构建，用于训练和评估支持科学灵感发现的检索系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献检索系统主要基于直接相关性匹配，无法捕捉科学研究中多样化的灵感获取方式。科学家在检索文献时，不仅需要直接解决某个问题的方案，还需要更一般化的视角（放大）或更具体的实现（缩小）。这种多层次的"灵感"检索需求尚未有系统化的基准来定义和评估。

Method: 作者提出RATIO基准，定义了三种"灵感移动"操作：Address（检索解决问题的潜在方法）、Broaden（检索更一般化的表述）、Specify（检索具体的实例化）。构建方法是将原本仅用于分类的discourse-marker远程监督扩展到语料规模的信息检索，结合LLM和人工审核，从数百万篇CS领域全文论文中构建。

Result: 实验表明，针对特定操作进行微调的检索模型相较于基础检索器有显著性能提升，但仍有很大的改进空间，说明RATIO是一个具有挑战性的基准。

Conclusion: RATIO为文献驱动的科学灵感检索提供了可扩展的训练和评估框架，开辟了科学灵感检索这一新兴研究方向。

Abstract: Retrieved scientific literature can serve as inspiration for both human and AI scientists. Inspiration can take different forms: prior work may directly suggest how to address a problem, or surface directions at different levels of abstraction - zooming out to a more general view or zooming in to a concrete realization. We introduce RATIO (Retrieval Across Typed Ideation Operations), a large-scale benchmark in which relevance is defined by three operations which we name ideation moves: Address retrieves potential approaches for stated problems, Broaden retrieves more general formulations, and Specify retrieves concrete instantiations. RATIO is constructed from millions of full-text scientific papers across CS literature via a general recipe that extends discourse-marker distant supervision - previously used only for classification - to corpus-scale retrieval, combined with extensive LLM and human vetting. Experiments show that operation-specific fine-tuning substantially boosts retrievers but leaves much room for further improvements. RATIO provides a scalable training and evaluation framework for retrieval components that support literature-grounded ideation, opening up new research avenues on scientific inspiration retrieval.

</details>


### [205] [Making Clinical Language Models Auditable: Concept-Guided Fine-Tuning for Robust Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27397)
*Jin Mu,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CAST是一个基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的临床文本分类框架，通过暴露和抑制笔记特有伪影（如模板、分隔符）来提升模型在部署场景下的鲁棒性和可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床语言模型在医院内部数据上表现良好，但在部署时会因利用笔记特有的伪影（如模板、分隔符、套话）而失效，这些伪影并不反映患者的真实状态。这导致模型在分布偏移下的泛化能力不足，且缺乏人类可理解的审计手段。

Method: CAST框架包含四个核心环节：(1) 使用稀疏自编码器从Transformer中间激活中提取稀疏、人类可审计的特征；(2) 通过LLM辅助的解读流程和ICD-10检索约束对SAE隐变量进行标注；(3) 在微调过程中通过残差减法抑制已验证的伪影隐变量；(4) 提供事后逐概念归因以实现模型决策的审计。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV出院记录死亡率预测任务上，CAST优于对应的微调编码器基线，并与强LLM基线保持竞争力；同时提供了特征级别的审计轨迹，展示支持每次预测的临床概念以及训练中被抑制的伪影概念。

Conclusion: CAST通过可审计的伪影抑制机制，在不牺牲性能的前提下增强了临床文本分类模型的鲁棒性，为临床语言模型在真实部署场景中的可解释性和可信度提供了新的解决方案。
